Theorem 228.1 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The discrete variety V defined by Optimality Condition constraints admits a stratification into 65 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 851 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The Optimality Condition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 65 + O(1/n^851). Summing over all modes and applying the discrete Optimality Condition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of Optimality Condition and the invariant on the space spanned by discrete generators. At step n, the Optimality Condition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 121. QED.
